# Personalized Investor Emails

Each email below is generated from the **actual content** of the corresponding pitch deck PPTX file.
Replace `[FIRST NAME]` with the actual contact name. Attach the **PDF version** of the deck.

**Total: 500 personalized emails**

---

### Andreessen Horowitz (a16z)
**Deck:** `investor-100/pptx/001-a16z.pptx`
*Venture Capital · Menlo Park, CA · $35B+*

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Andreessen Horowitz (a16z)

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Andreessen Horowitz (a16z):** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Databricks, Anyscale, Hex, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Andreessen Horowitz (a16z). Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

Best,
Stuart Rainey
Founder & CEO, Datacendia
contact@datacendia.com | datacendia.com | app.datacendia.com

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Aluminari Ventures:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Enterprise B2B, Anyscale, Hex, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Junction Ventures:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Pacific NW enterprise, Anyscale, Hex, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped

**Deck:** `investor-500/pptx/408-omers.pptx`
*Pension VC · Toronto, Canada · $5B+*

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Omers Ventures

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Omers Ventures:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Shopify, Hopper, Wealthsimple, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Omers Ventures. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

Best,
Stuart Rainey
Founder & CEO, Datacendia

**Deck:** `investor-500/pptx/425-puma-springboard.pptx`
*Venture Capital · Dublin, Ireland · $300M+*

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Puma Springboard

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Puma Springboard:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Anyscale, Hex, Replit — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Puma Springboard. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

Best,

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Shunwei Capital

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Shunwei Capital:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Chinese mobile and AI, Anyscale, Hex, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Shunwei Capital. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Tata Capital Healthcare Fund

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Tata Capital Healthcare Fund:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Anyscale, Hex, Replit — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Tata Capital Healthcare Fund. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Venture Reality Fund

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Venture Reality Fund:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Anyscale, Hex, Replit — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Venture Reality Fund. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

*Corporate VC · Salt Lake City, UT · $100M+*

**Subject:** Datacendia — Decision Evidence Infrastructure for AI Systems | Prepared for Zions Bancorporation Ventures

**Body:**

Hi [FIRST NAME],

I'm Stuart Rainey, founder of Datacendia. I've built the decision evidence layer for enterprise AI — the infrastructure that produces cryptographic proof of how AI-assisted decisions are made.

**Why Zions Bancorporation Ventures:** EU AI Act (2025–2026) → enforceable auditability requirements. DORA → operational traceability for financial services. Litigation risk → retrospective scrutiny of AI decisions. Enterprise AI adoption → exploding unaudited decision surface. This creates a new infrastructure requirement: decision evidence.

Your portfolio includes Banking enterprise tech, Anyscale, Hex, — companies building the data and AI stack. Datacendia completes that stack with decision evidence and regulatory compliance for every AI decision.

**What's built (not idea-stage):**
- 205K+ passing tests, 156 validated API endpoints, live production platform
- Multi-agent deliberation with cryptographic evidence (Ed25519, Merkle trees, RFC 3161)
- Sovereign deployment: cloud, private cloud, on-prem, air-gapped
- NVIDIA Inception member
- Active design-partner conversations with a financial institution and Big 4 channel

**The ask:** $1.5M pre-seed at $7M pre-money. Engineering risk is resolved — this is a GTM-stage investment. Funds go to first 3 design partners, SOC 2 Type II, and EU AI Act regulatory validation.

I've attached a deck personalized for Zions Bancorporation Ventures. Happy to schedule a 20-minute deep dive or live demo at your convenience.

Best,
Stuart Rainey
Founder & CEO, Datacendia
